Simulated annealing style:
- Start from binary start, compute Fmax.
- For proposes: random zero-one pair. Compute trial Fmax. Accept if <= current (swap), or with probability exp((curr-trial)/T) (annealing). Revert if rejected.
- Need fast trial: update R,U, compute max, store changes to revert. Instead of modifying arrays and reverting full O(n) (copy), we can apply and revert using negative deltas. Since dR and dU O(n) updates; to revert apply -delta. But we must be careful if accepted, keep; if rejected, undo. We can precompute delta arrays for a,b based on current u (before flipping). After accepted, u changes and delta for next uses new u.
- For trial, after updating R,U, max. If accepted, no revert. If rejected, apply negative deltas to R,U; revert u. This is 2 O(n) updates. Good.
- We can maintain u int8; flip a,b.

Implementation details for project_u:
```
def project_u(v):
    v=np.asarray(v)
    # if sum small and within bounds, waterfilling maybe λ=0; but need exact.
    lo = float(v.min()) - 2
    hi = float(v.max()) + 2
    # We need sum clip(v-lam)=0.
    # If all v within and sum ~0, λ≈0 but bisection still.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(v-mid,-1,1).sum()
       if s > 0: # need larger lam to reduce sum
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    u=np.clip(v-lam,-1,1)
    s=u.sum()
    if abs(s)>1e-1:
       # adjust one free
    return u
```
Need monotonic: sum decreases as lam increases. If s>0, need increase lam -> lo=mid. If s<0, hi=mid. Initially lo=min(v)-2 ensures clip all -1? sum=-n; hi=max(v)+2 sum=n. Good.
But if v has huge values, fine. Bisection 80 for precision. However if many variables inactive, s piecewise constant? There may be flat intervals where sum target not achieved? Since clip to -1/1 with n variables, as lam varies sum continuous from -n to n, target 0 always achieved. Good.
After bisection, due to rounding, adjust residual by modifying a free (u> -1+tol & u<1-tol). If no free, all ±1; sum of ±1 cannot be exactly 0 if n even? It can if equal counts. If all ±1 and sum not 0 due to odd? n even, but if all saturated with unequal counts, target not achieved? But projection would not have all saturated if target 0 possible; waterfilling leaves at least one free unless exact half counts. If no free, adjust maybe break bound tiny? We'll handle.

_G_N=0; _G_BETA=1.0; _G_U=None; _G_D=None; _G_GU=None

def project_to_u(v): ...

def d_values_and_grad_u(u, beta):
    n=_G_N
    R = np.correlate(u,u,'full')
    R = R[n-1:2*n-1]
    pref = np.cumsum(u)
    U = np.zeros(n)
    # for t=0: U=0; for t>=1 U[n-t-1]
    U[1:] = pref[n-2::-1]? Let's check: t=1 => m=n-1 -> U=pref[n-2]. index 1. t=n-1 => m=1 -> U=pref[0]. So U[1:] = pref[-1:0:-1] (pref[n-2] down to pref[0]).
    m = n - np.arange(n)
    D = (m.astype(float) - R + 2.0*np.abs(U))*0.25
    Dmax = np.max(D)
    shift = beta*(D-Dmax)
    exps = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -700, 0.0))
    sumexp = exps.sum()
    weights = exps/sumexp
    f = Dmax + np.log(sumexp)/beta
    gu = np.zeros(n)
    # t=0
    w0=weights[0]
    if w0>0: gu -= u * (w0/2.0)  # because -u/2
    for t in range(1,n):
       w=weights[t]
       if w <= 1e-300: continue
       m_t = n - t
       dR = np.zeros(n)
       dR[:m_t] += u[t:]  # length m_t
       dR[t:] += u[:m_t] # length m_t
       gu += -0.25 * w * dR
       Ut=U[t]
       if Ut > 1e-12: gu[:m_t] += 0.5*w
       elif Ut < -1e-12: gu[:m_t] -= 0.5*w
    return f, gu, D
```

def autocorr(u):

 while N < 2*n-1: N*=2

Binary local search on starts:
Function `binary_hillclimb(u, time_limit, rng, max_iter)`:
- n even, u int ±1 sum zero.
- Initialize R,U,D? Use compute_R_U(u) direct: R autocorr, U prefix. Since u ±1.

- Each iteration:
   if no ones/zeros break.
   a=rng.choice(ones); b=rng.choice(zeros)
   Compute delta for a=-2 (if u[a]=1) and b=+2.
   Original u[a]=1, u[b]=-1.
   Update arrays:

   Function apply_flip(pos, delta):
      # U: t=0..n-pos-1? Actually U_t for pos if pos < n-t => t < n-pos. t=0..n-pos-1. `U[:n-pos] += delta`
      # R: if t < n-pos: dR[t] += delta*u[pos+t] for t=1..n-pos-1 -> `R[1:n-pos] += delta*u[pos+1:n]`
          if pos>0: `R[pos+1:] += delta*u[:pos]`
   But for t=0, no R (autocorr R0 unchanged), U0? U[0] should be 0. Update U[:n-pos] includes U[0]; after both flips U0 remains 0. Good.
   Apply for a and b. Then newD = (m - R + 2absU)/4; newF=max. If accept: u flip, update lists (remove/add), currF=newF; if newF<best update best. If reject: apply -delta to revert (same function with -delta), u unchanged.
   Need when applying second flip, u changes? For trial, we want compute delta based on original u for both flips individually? The change in R due to flipping a and b is additive if they are distinct, because R after both = R_orig + δR_a(old) + δR_b(old) + cross? Wait R is quadratic; if we flip both, the contribution involving pair (a,b) changes in a way not captured by independent deltas based on old u if both changed. Suppose R=∑u_i u_j. Change when u_a and u_b both change: Δ = u_a u_b old -> new. The independent deltas sum: δ_a * (u_b_old + neighbors) + δ_b*(u_a_old+neighbors) = -2*(u_b +...) +2*(u_a+...). Does that equal full? For pair product u_a u_b, old= -1 (since +* -). new=+1 (+*+? a becomes -1, b becomes +1 -> -1) Wait swapping signs: a 1->-1, b -1->1. product old -1, new -1, unchanged. Independent: δ_a*u_b + δ_b*u_a = -2*(-1)+2*(1)=4? Actually δ_a=-2, u_b_old=-1 ->2; δ_b=2,u_a_old=1->2; sum 4, wrong (change 0). Because when both changed, the neighbor factor for a should be new u_b? Detailed code functions:

```
def compute_c5(h):
    if len(h)==0: return inf
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr))* (2.0/len(h))

def d_info(h, beta):
    n=_G_N
    u=2*h-1
    R=autocorr_nonneg(u)
    pref=np.cumsum(u)
    U=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1: U[1:]=pref[-1:0:-1]  # check length n-1
    m=n-np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    D=(m - R + 2*np.abs(U))*0.25
    Dmax=np.max(D)
    shift=beta*(D-Dmax)
    exps=np.exp(np.clip(shift,-700,0.0))
    sumexp=float(exps.sum())
    if sumexp<=0: return Dmax, np.zeros(n)
    weights=exps/sumexp
    f=Dmax + math.log(sumexp)/beta
    gu=np.zeros(n)
    for t in range(n):
       w=weights[t]
       if w < 1e-12: continue
       m_t=n-t
       a=-0.25*w
       # gu[:m] += a * u[t:t+m]
       gu[:m_t] += a * u[t:]
       gu[t:] += a * u[:m_t]
       Ut=U[t]
       if Ut > 1e-12:
           gu[:m_t] += 0.5*w
       elif Ut < -1e-12:
           gu[:m_t] -= 0.5*w
    return f, gu
```
Careful slice `u[t:]` length n-t=m. `u[:m_t]` length m. For t=0: u[t:] and u[:m] both full. Good.
The loop modifies gu with slices; for t=0, does gu += a*u twice -> -0.5 w u. Good.
If W small skip. But weights sum 1; for β low many weights. The Dmax plus logsum f may be not exact if β low.

Check U[1:] = pref[-1:0:-1]. For n=5 pref [p0..p4]. U[1] should p3 (m=4 -> sum first4), U[4] p0. `pref[-1:0:-1]` gives p4,p3,p2,p1 not p3..p0. We need exclude total p4=0. Use `pref[n-2::-1]`. For n=5, pref[3::-1] -> p3,p2,p1,p0. Good. For n=1, none. So:
```
if n>1: U[1:] = pref[n-2::-1]
```
U[0]=0. For t=n? none.

Autocorr method: Need for n maybe 1. implement:
```
def autocorr_nonneg(u):
    n=len(u)
    if n<512:
       c=np.correlate(u,u,mode='full')
       return c[n-1:2*n-1]
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    f=np.fft.rfft(u,N)
    ac=np.fft.irfft(f*np.conj(f), N)
    return ac[:n].copy()
```
Will FFT result contain tiny negative zeros. Could `ac[:n]`. For n=512, N=1024 (since 2n-1=1023). Good.

def project_h(v,target,iters=50):

   if np.all((v>=0)&(v<=1)):
      s=v.sum()
      if abs(s-target)<1e-12: return v.copy()
   # bisection

After h, adjust residual:
```
s=h.sum()-target
if abs(s)>1e-13:
  free=np.where((h>1e-10)&(h<1-1e-10))[0]
  if free.size:
     # distribute to one with enough room? Just subtract s from first free, clip
     idx=free[0]
     # if adjustment exceeds bounds, spread
     need=s
     for idx in free:
        if need>0:
           cut=np.minimum(1.0-h[idx], need); h[idx]+=cut; need-=cut
        else:
           cut=np.minimum(h[idx], -need); h[idx]-=cut; need+=cut
        if abs(need)<1e-14: break
  else:
     # clip and adjust by tiny uniform maybe breaks bounds; use h = h - s/n; clip
     for _ in range(5): h=np.clip(h - (h.sum()-target)/n,0,1)
```

Binary local search function. Need compute R,U for u int. Use autocorr helper (float/int? Use float for simplicity). U = prefix. For binary, R integer but float. D = (m - R + 2absU)/4. F = max(D) (could be float .0). We can maintain `F = np.max(D)`.
Apply flip:
```
def binary_apply_flip(u, R, U, pos, delta):
    # delta = +2 or -2
    n=_G_N
    # U: for t=0..n-pos-1? pos < n - t -> t < n-pos. t indexes 0..n-pos-1 inclusive count n-pos.
    if n - pos > 0:
        U[:n-pos] += delta
    # R for t where pos+t<n (t=1..n-pos-1)
    end = n - pos
    if end > 1:
        R[1:end] += delta * u[pos+1:n]
    if pos > 0:
        R[pos+1:] += delta * u[:pos]
```
But for t=0, R0 should remain sum u^2; flipping ±1 leaves unchanged? Each u^2=1, yes. U0 changes by delta but total after two flips cancels. D0 formula with U0? We set U[0]=0 for D; if we update U[0], it can become nonzero during trial after first flip, but after second flips should 0. For D computation after both, U0 should 0; but if due to float, okay. However in binary_apply_flip for first flip, U0 changes. D intermediate not used. For second flip, U0 returns 0. Good. But if we compute D after both, U0 might be 0. In D formula, U[0] should be 0; if small residual, ignore or force U[0]=0 before max. Do `U[0]=0.0` when computing F.
R0: Our R array from autocorr includes R0. We don't update R0; since u^2 unchanged, fine. But R0 should be n. If float initial exact, yes.
Check R update conditions: For a single flip at pos, t positive. Condition first: base i=pos < m=n-t => t < n-pos. t from 1 to n-pos-1 (if end=n-pos, R[1:end]). New value of u[pos+t] before flip. Use u current. Good.
Second: shifted index i+t=pos => i=pos-t >=0 => t>pos. i<m automatically since i< n-t? pos-t < n-t iff pos<n true. So t=pos+1..n-1: R pos+1:n length n-pos-1, add delta*u[0:pos]. Good.
For pos=0: R[1:end] end=n, R[1:n] += delta*u[1:n]; no second. For pos=n-1: end=1 no first, second R[n:] none? pos+1=n, no; flipping last element affects no R for t? Pairs where last is shifted base? If t>pos none; as base none; indeed no pair with last at positive shift? It pairs with none. But U for t<1: U[0] only? If pos=n-1, n-pos=1, U[:1] changed; U_t for t>=1 does not include last. Good.
For a pair of flips, order matters due to cross, sequential handles. Use current u before each apply. We need update `u[pos] += delta` in apply? We can either update after applying deltas? The delta formula for R uses u of other positions, not the flipped position, but U no u. If we update u before applying second, correct. For reversal, update. Let's implement apply_flip modifying u and R,U:
```
def bin_flip(u,R,U,pos,delta):
    n=_G_N
    end=n-pos
    if end>0: U[:end] += delta
    if end>1: R[1:end] += delta*u[pos+1:n]
    if pos>0: R[pos+1:] += delta*u[:pos]
    u[pos] += delta

`project_h` implementation in detail:
```
def project_h(v, target, iters=50):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0: return v.copy()
    # if already feasible enough
    if np.all((v >= -1e-15) & (v <= 1.0 + 1e-15)):
        vc = np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
        s = float(vc.sum())
        if abs(s-target) <= 1e-12:
            return vc
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 2.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 2.0
    for _ in range(iters):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = float(np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    lam = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    h = np.clip(v - lam, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        free = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10))
        if free.size:
            # distribute diff over free indices greedily for safety
            for idx in free:
                if diff > 0:
                    add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
                    h[idx] += add
                    diff -= add
                else:
                    sub = min(h[idx], -diff)
                    h[idx] -= sub
                    diff += sub
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
                    break
        else:
            # adjust uniformly with clipping, a few iterations
            for _ in range(10):
                diff = target - float(h.sum())
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-15: break
                h = np.clip(h + diff / n, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
If free distribution leaves residual due to bounds? free size maybe but diff large? diff from bisection tiny; one free enough. Greedy fine. If diff not zero, loop free all. At end maybe residual small.

For objective calls, the free adjust can be costly if diff rarely >1e-14; okay. But bisection could produce diff 1e-13; free adjust loops maybe. We can reduce tol to 1e-13. Use `if abs(diff) > 1e-12`. In objective, 45 bisection enough. Good.

`autocorr_nonneg` with FFT:
```
def autocorr_nonneg(u):
    n=len(u)
    if n < 384:
        c=np.correlate(u,u,'full')
        return c[n-1:2*n-1].astype(float)
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    f=np.fft.rfft(u,N)
    ac=np.fft.irfft(f*np.conj(f), N)
    return ac[:n].copy()
```
For u int64 binary, `np.correlate` returns int? Cast float. Good.

`d_info_h`:
```
def d_info_h(h, beta):
    n=_G_N
    u = 2.0*h - 1.0
    R = autocorr_nonneg(u)
    pref = np.cumsum(u)
    U = np.zeros(n)
    if n > 1:
        U[1:] = pref[n-2::-1]
    m = (n - np.arange(n)).astype(float)
    D = (m - R + 2.0*np.abs(U))*0.25
    Dmax = float(np.max(D))
    shift = beta*(D - Dmax)
    exps = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -740.0, 0.0))
    sumexp = float(exps.sum())
    if sumexp <= 0.0:
        return Dmax, np.zeros(n)
    weights = exps / sumexp
    val = Dmax + math.log(sumexp) / beta
    gu = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    # maybe threshold
    for t in range(n):
       w = float(weights[t])
       if w < 1e-13: continue
       mt = n - t
       a = -0.25 * w
       gu[:mt] += a * u[t:]
       gu[t:] += a * u[:mt]
       Ut = U[t]
       if Ut > 1e-11:
           gu[:mt] += 0.5 * w
       elif Ut < -1e-11:
           gu[:mt] -= 0.5 * w
    return val, gu
```
Wait `u[t:]` length n-t=mt. Good. But `gu[t:]` length n-t=mt, `u[:mt]` length mt. For t=0, mt=n, u[t:] length n. Good.
Potential memory: `a*u[t:]` creates array each slice. Could be okay.

`smooth_obj_z`:
```
def smooth_obj_z(z):
    n=_G_N
    target=0.5*n
    h=project_h(z, target, 45)
    f, gu = d_info_h(h, _G_BETA)
    gh = gu * 2.0
    gh -= gh.mean()
    return f, gh
```
`gh` modifies gu but okay. If gh is view? `gu*2.0` new. `gh -= mean`.

One concern: L-BFGS-B may evaluate objective with `z` not 1D? It passes `np.atleast_2d`? Usually 1D. If 2D, len error. Fine.

`optimize_from_start`:
```
def optimize_from_start(h0, n, time_limit, rng):
    global _G_N,_G_BETA
    _G_N=n
    target=0.5*n
    h=project_h(h0,target,60)
    c=compute_c5(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)] for ...
    # schedules based on n
    if n <=320: betas=[10,30,100,300,1000]; maxit=400
    elif n <=640: betas=[10,30,100,300,1000]; maxit=250
    else: betas=[20,60,200,600]; maxit=150
    z=h.copy()
    for beta in betas:
       if remaining_time() < 15: break
       _G_BETA=float(beta)
       try:
           res = minimize(smooth_obj_z, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'iprint':0})
       except Exception:
           res=None
       if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
           z2=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
           if np.all(np.isfinite(z2)):
               z2 = np.clip(z2,-3.0,3.0)
               z2 -= z2.mean()
               h2=project_h(z2,target,70)
               c2=compute_c5(h2)
               if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                   best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); z=h2.copy()
               else:
                   # still maybe use z2 if it is not too bad? Use h2 to not stay? Maybe if smooth objective improved. We don't know. Use z2 if c2 < c + 1e-8?
                   # For high beta, local optimum could have exact slightly worse but better basin? To be safe, z = h2 if c2<=c+1e-8 else keep z.
                   if c2 <= c + 1e-9:
                       z = h2.copy()
                   # else keep z
       # After each beta, maybe if no improvement but smooth better? ignore.
    # Final exact projected gradient? optional
    return best_h,best_c
```
If L-BFGS maxiter large, may exceed time_limit. We rely remaining. But one minimize call may take >time_limit. Need pass a callback to check time? L-BFGS-B callback can raise? We can create top-level `time_callback`? It receives x. It can raise Timeout? Use exception to abort. Define `class TimeOut?` no class? Could use custom exception? Easier: set maxiter based on time and assume okay. But a single call could hang if infeasible. Use `maxiter` small. For safety, use callback to check remaining and if <5 set flag? L-BFGS callback cannot stop except returning negative? In scipy, callback can raise. Define top-level `_G_OPT_STOP` global bool and callback:
```
def _opt_callback(x):
    if remaining_time() < 10.0:
        raise KeyboardInterrupt? # not ideal
```
Raising `RuntimeError` inside minimize may propagate? It will raise. We can set `_G_STOP=True` and in callback if stop: raise _OptTimeout. Define class? A top-level exception class is okay. But no closures. Use:

`smooth_value_h(h,beta)` calls d_info_h returns f,gu. We can compute `g = 2*gu - mean` as feasible gradient. Then:
```
def polish_pg(h,n,beta,iter_max,time_limit):
  _G_N=n; target=n/2; h=project_h(h,target,70)
  f_val,gu = d_info_h(h,beta)
  g = 2*gu; g -= g.mean()
  cur_f=f_val; cur_c=compute_c5(h)
  for it in range(iter_max):
     if remaining_time() < 5: break
     trial = h
     step = 0.2
     for ls in range(30):
        trial = project_h(h - step*g, target,50)
        f_tri,_ = d_info_h(trial,beta)
        if f_tri < cur_f - 1e-13:
            h=trial; cur_f=f_tri; break
        step*=0.5
     else: break
     if it%5==0:
        c=compute_c5(h)
        if c<best...
  return best
```

Binary search implementation more concretely:

```
def binary_search(h0,n,time_limit,rng):
    global _G_N
    _G_N=n
    target=n//2
    # convert h0 to balanced binary by top target u
    u = 2*h0-1
    idx = np.argsort(u)[::-1][:target]
    ub = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    ub[idx] = 1
    # ensure sum zero? If target n/2, sum ub = n/2, u = 2ub-1 sum0.
    u = ub*2-1
    R = np.correlate(u,u,'full')[n-1:2*n-1].astype(np.int64)
    pref = np.cumsum(u).astype(np.int64)
    U = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
    if n>1: U[1:] = pref[n-2::-1]
    m = (n - np.arange(n)).astype(np.int64)
    def compute_val():
       U[0]=0
       val = m - R + 2*np.abs(U)
       return int(np.max(val))
    curr_val = compute_val()
    best_val=curr_val; best_u=u.copy()
    # lists
    ones = [int(i) for i in np.where(u==1)[0]]
    zeros = [int(i) for i in np.where(u==-1)[0]]
    t0=time.time(); T0=4.0; max_iter=int(time_limit*15000) maybe.
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if not ones or not zeros: break
       if (it & 1023)==0:
          if time.time()-t0 > time_limit or remaining_time()<15: break
       ia = rng.randint(len(ones)); ib=rng.randint(len(zeros))
       a=ones[ia]; b=zeros[ib]
       # trial
       bin_flip(u,R,U,a,-2)
       bin_flip(u,R,U,b,2)
       U[0]=0
       new_val = int(np.max(m - R + 2*np.abs(U)))
       accept=False
       if new_val < curr_val: accept=True
       elif new_val == curr_val and rng.random() < 0.3: accept=True
       else:
          frac = (time.time()-t0)/max(1e-6,time_limit)
          T = T0*(1.0-frac)
          if T > 0.01:
              d = float(new_val-curr_val)
              if d < 8.0 or rng.random() < math.exp(-d/T): accept=True
       if accept:
          curr_val = new_val
          if new_val < best_val:
              best_val = new_val; best_u = u.copy()
          # update lists
          ones[ia] = b
          zeros[ib] = zeros[-1]
          zeros.pop()
          zeros.append(a)
       else:
          bin_flip(u,R,U,b,-2)
          bin_flip(u,R,U,a,2)
          U[0]=0
    # optional final deterministic tiny: maybe sort by val? no
    h=(0.5*(best_u+1)).astype(float)
    h=project_h(h,n/2.0,70)
    return h, float(best_val)/4.0 * (2.0/n)
```
Need `bin_flip` top-level uses _G_N and modifies arrays. It assumes u[pos] currently ±1. It updates R using current u before flip. But after first flip, the neighbor values include u[a] changed; good. For second, if b was neighbor of a, uses new u[a]. Correct. If reject, revert in reverse order: flip b back (-2), then a back (+2). But since u changed, rollback exact. Need after rollback, U[0]=0.
The `bin_flip` R update uses `u[pos+1:n]` etc. If update `u[pos] += delta` after R, for the same flip no issue. For second flip, `u` has first change. For reversal, apply b -2 uses current u with a flipped; then a +2 uses current u with b reverted, but a's neighbors maybe b reverted, matching original? Let's test cross pair: original a=1,b=-1. Forward: flip a to -1; R cross ab unchanged? Independent sequential: first flip a delta -2, neighbor b=-1 contributes +2 to R for t where adjacent; second flip b +2, neighbor a=-1 contributes -2; total 0. Reverse: state a=-1,b=1. Flip b -2: neighbor a=-1 contributes +2; flip a +2: neighbor b=-1 contributes -2; total 0; original R restored. Good.
Need if a,b not paired, additive. Good.

`bin_flip` with int8 u: `delta * u[...]` where u int8 and delta int gives int64? Numpy may promote. R int64. Ensure u[pos] = np.int8(np.clip(u[pos]+delta,-1,1))? Adding delta to int8 +2 from -1 ->1 ok; from 1 to -1 = -1; no overflow if int8? 1 + (-2) = -1; -1+2=1; safe. Good.

```
def threshold_h(h,n):
    u=2.0*h-1.0
    target=n//2
    idx=np.argsort(u)[::-1][:target]
    out=np.zeros(n); out[idx]=1.0
    return out
```

Implement `polish_projected_gradient(h,n,time_limit,beta=500)`:
```
def polish_projected_gradient(h,n,time_limit,beta=500):
    global _G_N,_G_BETA
    _G_N=n; _G_BETA=float(beta)
    target=0.5*n
    h=project_h(h,target,70)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h)
    t0=time.time()
    f,gu = d_info_h(h,beta)
    cur_f=f
    g = 2*gu; g -= g.mean()
    for it in range(2000):
       if time.time()-t0 > time_limit or remaining_time()<15: break
       step=0.1
       improved=False
       for ls in range(25):
           trial = project_h(h - step*g, target,50)
           f_tri,gu_tri = d_info_h(trial,beta)
           if f_tri < cur_f - 1e-13:
               h=trial; cur_f=f_tri; g=2*gu_tri; g-=g.mean(); improved=True; break
           step *=0.5
       if not improved: break
       if it % 10 == 0:
           c=compute_c5(h)
           if c < best_c - 1e-12:
               best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```

Detailed `optimize_from_start`:
```
def optimize_from_start(h0, n, time_limit, rng, betas=None):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA, _G_CALL_START, _G_CALL_LIMIT
    n = int(n)
    if n <=0: return h0, compute_c5(h0)
    if n % 2 ==1: n +=1
    _G_N = n
    target = 0.5*n
    h = project_h(h0, target, 60)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    if not np.isfinite(c): ... default? 
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    if betas is None:
       if n <=320: betas = (10.0,30.0,100.0,300.0,1000.0)
       else: betas = (10.0,30.0,100.0,300.0)
    bounds = [(-3.0,3.0)] * n
    t0 = time.time()
    nb = len(betas)
    h = h.copy()
    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if remaining_time() < 30 or time.time()-t0 > max(10.0, time_limit): break
        call_limit = max(5.0, (time_limit / float(nb)) * 1.5)
        call_limit = min(call_limit, max(5.0, remaining_time()-20.0))
        _G_BETA = float(beta)
        _G_CALL_START = time.time()
        _G_CALL_LIMIT = call_limit
        maxiter = 300 if n<=320 else 180
        if beta >= 1000: maxiter = 150
        try:
            res = minimize(smooth_obj_z, h, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, callback=_opt_callback, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':20,'disp':False})
        except OptTimeout:
            res = None
        except Exception:
            res = None
        if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
            z2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if z2.shape != (n,): z2 = z2.reshape(n)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(z2)):
                z2 = np.clip(z2, -3.0, 3.0)
                z2 -= z2.mean()
                h2 = project_h(z2, target, 70)
                c2 = compute_c5(h2)
                if np.isfinite(c2):
                    if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = c2; best_h = h2.copy()
                        h = h2.copy()
                    elif c2 <= c + 1e-7:
                        h = h2.copy()
                    # else keep h
        if time.time()-t0 > time_limit*0.9: break
    # maybe final project and exact
    h_f = project_h(best_h, target, 80)
    c_f=compute_c5(h_f)
    if np.isfinite(c_f) and c_f < best_c:
        best_c=c_f; best_h=h_f.copy()
    return best_h, best_c
```
Use `h` as z start. If exact not improve but c2 slightly worse, we keep h2 for next beta if c2 <= c+1e-7. This may wander. Good.
`maxls` option available. Fine.

    target=n//2
    u_h=2.0*h0-1.0
    idx=np.argsort(u_h)[::-1][:target]
    ub=np.ones(n,dtype=np.int8); ub[idx]=? Wait h0 values; to select top as ones: initialize -1; set idx 1.

def polish_slsqp(h0,n,time_limit,betas):

      except: pass
      if res ...:
         h2=project_h(res.x,target,70); c2=...
         if c2 < best_c: update; h=h2
   return best...
```

```
def normalize_start(h0,n):
   n=int(n)
   if n%2: n+=1
   if len(h0)!=n: h0=interpolate_h(h0,n)
   else: h0=h0.copy()
   return project_h(h0,0.5*n,50), n
```

def force_exact_sum(h):
    target=0.5*len(h)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0).copy()
    for _ in range(10):
        diff=target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
        free=np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))
        if free.size:
            # choose index with enough room
            idx=free[0]
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
            else:
                add = -min(h[idx], -diff)
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            # all saturated; toggle one if necessary
            if diff > 0:
                idx=int(np.argmin(h))
                h[idx]=1.0
            else:
                idx=int(np.argmax(h))
                h[idx]=0.0
            break
    h = project_h(h, target, 5) # maybe not, could change again
    # final adjust one free without project
    diff=target - float(h.sum())
    free=...
    if free.size and abs(diff)>1e-15: h[free[0]] = float(np.clip(h[free[0]] + diff,0,1))
    return h
```

We'll implement `force_exact_sum` top-level:
```
def force_exact_sum(h):
    target=0.5*len(h)
    h=np.asarray(h,float).clip(0,1)
    for _ in range(5):
       free = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))
       if free.size == 0:
           diff=target-h.sum()
           if diff>0 and np.any(h<1.0-1e-12): h[int(np.argmin(h))]=1.0
           elif diff<0 and np.any(h>1e-12): h[int(np.argmax(h))]=0.0
           break
       # pick free with largest tolerance in direction
       if target > h.sum():
           idx=int(np.argmax(1.0 - h[free]))
           idx=free[idx]
           good = h[idx] < 1.0
           # set to expose
           others=float(np.sum(h))-float(h[idx])
           new=target-others
           if 0.0 <= new <= 1.0: h[idx]=new
           else: h[idx]=np.clip(new,0,1)
       else:
           idx=int(np.argmax(h[free]))
           idx=free[idx]
           others=float(np.sum(h))-float(h[idx])
           new=target-others
           if 0.0 <= new <= 1.0: h[idx]=new
           else: h[idx]=np.clip(new,0,1)
       if abs(target - float(h.sum())) <= 1e-15: break
    return h
```
This tries to make exact. If new not feasible due to target-others, clips and loop. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


_G_N = 0
_G_BETA = 1.0
_G_START = 0.0
_G_BUDGET = 1300.0
_G_MARGIN = 10.0
_G_CALL_START = 0.0
_G_CALL_LIMIT = 60.0


class OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass


def remaining_time():
    return max(0.0, _G_BUDGET - (time.time() - _G_START) - _G_MARGIN)


def _opt_callback(x):
    if remaining_time() < 12.0:
        raise OptTimeout()
    if time.time() - _G_CALL_START > _G_CALL_LIMIT:
        raise OptTimeout()


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    val = float(np.max(corr))
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return float('inf')
    return val * (2.0 / float(n))


def project_h(v, target, iters=50):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()

    if np.all((v >= -1e-15) & (v <= 1.0 + 1e-15)):
        vc = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(float(vc.sum()) - target) <= 1e-12:
            return vc

    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 2.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 2.0
    target = float(target)

    for _ in range(iters):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(v - lam, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        free = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10))
        if free.size:
            for idx in free:
                if diff > 0.0:
                    add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
                    h[idx] += add
                    diff -= add
                else:
                    sub = min(h[idx], -diff)
                    h[idx] -= sub
                    diff += sub
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
                    break
        else:
            for _ in range(8):
                diff = target - float(h.sum())
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
                    break
                h = np.clip(h + diff / n, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def force_exact_sum(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(8):
        free = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))
        if free.size == 0:
            diff = target - float(h.sum())
            if diff > 0.0:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                h[idx] = 1.0
            elif diff < 0.0:
                idx = int(np.argmax(h))
                h[idx] = 0.0
            else:
                break
        else:
            if target > float(h.sum()):
                idx = free[int(np.argmax(1.0 - h[free]))]
            else:
                idx = free[int(np.argmax(h[free]))]
            others = float(h.sum()) - float(h[idx])
            new_val = target - others
            h[idx] = float(np.clip(new_val, 0.0, 1.0))

        if abs(target - float(h.sum())) <= 1e-15:
            break
    return h


def autocorr_nonneg(u):
    n = len(u)
    if n < 512:
        c = np.correlate(u, u, mode='full')
        return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1].astype(float)

    N = 1
    while N < 2 * n - 1:
        N <<= 1
    f = np.fft.rfft(u, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(f * np.conj(f), N)
    return ac[:n].copy()


def d_info_h(h, beta):
    n = _G_N
    u = 2.0 * h - 1.0
    R = autocorr_nonneg(u)

    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    if n:
        pref[1:] = np.cumsum(u)

    t_arr = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    V = pref[n - t_arr] + pref[t_arr]
    if n > 0:
        V[0] = 0.0

    m = (n - np.arange(n, dtype=float))
    D = (m - R + np.abs(V)) * 0.25
    Dmax = float(np.max(D))

    if not math.isfinite(Dmax):
        return float('inf'), np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    shift = beta * (D - Dmax)
    exps = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -740.0, 0.0))
    sumexp = float(exps.sum())
    if sumexp <= 0.0:
        return Dmax, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    weights = exps / sumexp
    val = Dmax + math.log(sumexp) / beta

    gu = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for t in range(n):
        w = float(weights[t])
        if w < 1e-13:
            continue

        mt = n - t
        a = -0.25 * w

        gu[:mt] += a * u[t:]
        gu[t:] += a * u[:mt]

        Vt = V[t]
        if Vt > 1e-11:
            s = 0.25 * w
            gu[:mt] += s
            if t > 0:
                gu[:t] += s
        elif Vt < -1e-11:
            s = -0.25 * w
            gu[:mt] += s
            if t > 0:
                gu[:t] += s

    return val, gu


def smooth_obj_z(z):
    n = _G_N
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = project_h(z, target, 45)
    f, gu = d_info_h(h, _G_BETA)
    if not math.isfinite(f):
        return f, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    gh = 2.0 * gu
    gh -= gh.mean()
    return f, gh


def precondition_candidate(h):
    n = len(h)
    h = project_h(h, 0.5 * float(n), 70)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    if math.isfinite(c):
        return h, c
    return h, float('inf')


def interpolate_h(src_h, dst_n):
    src_h = np.asarray(src_h, dtype=float)
    src_n = len(src_h)
    dst_n = int(dst_n)
    if dst_n % 2 == 1:
        dst_n += 1
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return project_h(src_h.copy(), 0.5 * dst_n, 60)

    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / src_n)
    xn = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / dst_n)
    h = np.interp(xn, xp, src_h)
    return project_h(h, 0.5 * dst_n, 60)


def threshold_h(h0, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    if len(h0) != n:
        h0 = interpolate_h(h0, n)
    else:
        h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    u = 2.0 * h0 - 1.0
    target = n // 2
    idx = np.argsort(u)[::-1][:target]
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    out[idx] = 1.0
    return out


def make_random_h(rng, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    out[rng.permutation(n)[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return out


def make_tm_h(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    for i in range(n):
        v = i
        p = 0
        while v:
            p ^= (v & 1)
            v >>= 1
        u[i] = 1 if (p & 1) else -1

    target = n // 2
    plus = int(np.sum(u == 1))
    if plus > target:
        need = plus - target
        idx = np.flatnonzero(u == 1)
        choose = None
        if idx.size:
            choose = rng.choice(idx, size=min(need, idx.size), replace=False)
        if choose is not None:
            u[choose] = -1
    elif plus < target:
        need = target - plus
        idx = np.flatnonzero(u == -1)
        choose = None
        if idx.size:
            choose = rng.choice(idx, size=min(need, idx.size), replace=False)
        if choose is not None:
            u[choose] = 1

    h = 0.5 * (u.astype(float) + 1.0)
    return project_h(h, 0.5 * n, 50)


def make_golden_h(rng, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    gr = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    for i in range(n):
        fr = (i * gr) % 1.0
        u[i] = 1 if fr < 0.5 else -1

    target = n // 2
    plus = int(np.sum(u == 1))
    if plus > target:
        need = plus - target
        idx = np.flatnonzero(u == 1)
        if idx.size:
            choose = rng.choice(idx, size=min(need, idx.size), replace=False)
            u[choose] = -1
    elif plus < target:
        need = target - plus
        idx = np.flatnonzero(u == -1)
        if idx.size:
            choose = rng.choice(idx, size=min(need, idx.size), replace=False)
            u[choose] = 1

    h = 0.5 * (u.astype(float) + 1.0)
    return project_h(h, 0.5 * n, 50)


def make_sinusoidal_h(rng, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    freq = int(rng.randint(1, 5))
    phase = float(rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi)
    h = 0.5 + 0.95 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + phase)
    return project_h(h, 0.5 * n, 55)


def bin_flip(u, R, V, pos, delta):
    n = _G_N
    end = n - pos

    if end > 0:
        V[:end] += delta
    if pos > 0:
        V[pos + 1:] += delta

    if end > 1:
        R[1:end] += delta * u[pos + 1:n]
    if pos > 0:
        R[pos + 1:] += delta * u[:pos]

    u[pos] = np.int8(u[pos] + delta)


def binary_search(h0, n, time_limit, rng):
    global _G_N

    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    _G_N = n

    h_bin = threshold_h(h0, n)
    u = (2.0 * h_bin - 1.0).astype(np.int8)

    R = np.correlate(u.astype(np.int64), u.astype(np.int64), mode='full')[n - 1:2 * n - 1].astype(np.int64)

    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    pref[0] = 0
    if n:
        pref[1:] = np.cumsum(u.astype(np.int64))

    t_arr = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    V = pref[n - t_arr] + pref[t_arr]
    V[0] = 0

    m = (n - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64))

    val_arr = m - R + np.abs(V)
    curr_val = int(np.max(val_arr))
    best_val = curr_val
    best_u = u.copy()

    ones = [int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(u == 1)]
    zeros = [int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(u == -1)]

    if not ones or not zeros:
        h = project_h(h_bin, 0.5 * n, 60)
        return h, compute_c5(h)

    t0 = time.time()
    max_iter = int(max(2000, time_limit * (9000.0 if n <= 320 else 2500.0)))
    max_iter = min(max_iter, 2000000)
    T0 = 4.0

    for it in range(max_iter):
        if (it & 1023) == 0:
            if time.time() - t0 > time_limit or remaining_time() < 20.0:
                break
        if not ones or not zeros:
            break

        ia = int(rng.randint(len(ones)))
        ib = int(rng.randint(len(zeros)))
        a = ones[ia]
        b = zeros[ib]

        bin_flip(u, R, V, a, -2)
        bin_flip(u, R, V, b, 2)
        V[0] = 0

        val_arr = m - R + np.abs(V)
        new_val = int(np.max(val_arr))

        accept = False
        if new_val < curr_val:
            accept = True
        elif new_val == curr_val:
            if rng.random() < 0.30:
                accept = True
        else:
            frac = (time.time() - t0) / max(1e-9, time_limit)
            T = T0 * max(0.0, 1.0 - frac)
            if T > 0.01:
                d = float(new_val - curr_val)
                if d < T * 3.0:
                    if rng.random() < math.exp(-d / T):
                        accept = True

        if accept:
            curr_val = new_val
            if new_val < best_val:
                best_val = new_val
                best_u = u.copy()

            ones[ia] = b
            zeros[ib] = zeros[-1]
            zeros.pop()
            zeros.append(a)
        else:
            bin_flip(u, R, V, b, -2)
            bin_flip(u, R, V, a, 2)
            V[0] = 0

    best_h = project_h(0.5 * (best_u.astype(float) + 1.0), 0.5 * n, 70)
    return best_h, compute_c5(best_h)


def optimize_from_start(h0, n, time_limit, rng, betas=None):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA, _G_CALL_START, _G_CALL_LIMIT

    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    _G_N = n
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    if len(h0) != n:
        h = interpolate_h(h0, n)
    else:
        h = project_h(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float), target, 60)

    c = compute_c5(h)
    if not math.isfinite(c):
        h = make_random_h(rng, n)
        c = compute_c5(h)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c

    if betas is None:
        if n <= 320:
            betas = (10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0)
        else:
            betas = (10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0)

    bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)] * n
    t0 = time.time()
    nb = len(betas)

    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if remaining_time() < 30.0 or time.time() - t0 > max(10.0, time_limit):
            break

        call_limit = max(5.0, (time_limit / float(nb)) * 1.4)
        call_limit = min(call_limit, max(5.0, remaining_time() - 20.0))

        _G_BETA = float(beta)
        _G_CALL_START = time.time()
        _G_CALL_LIMIT = call_limit

        maxiter = 320 if n <= 320 else 180
        if beta >= 1000.0:
            maxiter = min(maxiter, 180)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_obj_z,
                h,
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                callback=_opt_callback,
                options={
                    'maxiter': maxiter,
                    'ftol': 1e-12,
                    'gtol': 1e-7,
                    'maxls': 20,
                    'disp': False
                }
            )
        except OptTimeout:
            res = None
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, 'x'):
            z2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if z2.size == n:
                z2 = z2.reshape(n)
            else:
                z2 = None

            if z2 is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(z2)):
                z2 = np.clip(z2, -3.0, 3.0)
                z2 -= z2.mean()
                h2 = project_h(z2, target, 70)
                c2 = compute_c5(h2)
                if math.isfinite(c2):
                    if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = c2
                        best_h = h2.copy()
                        h = h2.copy()
                    elif c2 <= c + 1e-7:
                        h = h2.copy()

        if time.time() - t0 > 0.95 * time_limit:
            break

    h_f = project_h(best_h, target, 80)
    c_f = compute_c5(h_f)
    if math.isfinite(c_f) and c_f < best_c:
        best_c = c_f
        best_h = h_f.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def obj_h_only(h):
    f, gu = d_info_h(h, _G_BETA)
    return f


def jac_h_only(h):
    f, gu = d_info_h(h, _G_BETA)
    g = 2.0 * gu
    return g - g.mean()


def h_sum_fun(h):
    return float(np.sum(h)) - 0.5 * _G_N


def h_sum_jac(h):
    return np.ones(_G_N, dtype=float)


def polish_slsqp(h0, n, time_limit, betas):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA, _G_CALL_START, _G_CALL_LIMIT

    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    _G_N = n
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    h = project_h(h0, target, 70)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': h_sum_fun, 'jac': h_sum_jac}]
    t0 = time.time()

    for beta in betas:
        if remaining_time() < 25.0 or time.time() - t0 > time_limit:
            break

        _G_BETA = float(beta)
        _G_CALL_START = time.time()
        _G_CALL_LIMIT = min(60.0, max(8.0, time_limit / 3.0))

        maxiter = 120 if n <= 320 else 70
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_h_only,
                h,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=constraints,
                jac=jac_h_only,
                callback=_opt_callback,
                options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'iprint': 0}
            )
        except OptTimeout:
            res = None
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, 'x'):
            x2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if x2.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(x2)):
                h2 = project_h(x2, target, 80)
                c2 = compute_c5(h2)
                if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = h2.copy()
                    h = h2.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def polish_projected_gradient(h0, n, time_limit, beta=800.0):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA

    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n += 1
    _G_N = n
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    _G_BETA = float(beta)

    h = project_h(h0, target, 80)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)

    t0 = time.time()
    cur_f, gu = d_info_h(h, beta)
    if not math.isfinite(cur_f):
        return best_h, best_c
    g = 2.0 * gu
    g -= g.mean()

    for _ in range(3000):
        if time.time() - t0 > time_limit or remaining_time() < 18.0:
            break

        step = 0.1
        improved = False
        for _ls in range(28):
            trial = project_h(h - step * g, target, 50)
            f_tri, gu_tri = d_info_h(trial, beta)
            if math.isfinite(f_tri) and f_tri < cur_f - 1e-13:
                h = trial
                cur_f = f_tri
                g = 2.0 * gu_tri
                g -= g.mean()
                improved = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            break

        if _ % 8 == 0:
            c = compute_c5(h)
            if math.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _G_START, _G_BUDGET, _G_MARGIN

    _G_START = time.time()
    _G_BUDGET = float(budget_s)
    _G_MARGIN = 10.0

    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    try:
        parent_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    if parent_h is None or len(parent_h) < 2:
        parent_h = make_random_h(rng, 256)

    parent_n = len(parent_h)
    if parent_n % 2 == 1:
        parent_n += 1
        parent_h = interpolate_h(parent_h, parent_n)

    best_h = project_h(parent_h, 0.5 * float(parent_n), 80)
    best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
    best_n = len(best_h)

    if not math.isfinite(best_c5):
        best_h = make_random_h(rng, 256)
        best_n = len(best_h)
        best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    print("start: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

    first_n = best_n
    if first_n < 160:
        first_n = 256
    elif first_n > 640:
        first_n = 512
    if first_n % 2 == 1:
        first_n += 1

    if best_n != first_n:
        best_first, c_first = precondition_candidate(interpolate_h(best_h, first_n))
        best_first_n = len(best_first)
    else:
        best_first = project_h(best_h, 0.5 * float(first_n), 80)
        c_first = compute_c5(best_first)
        best_first_n = first_n

    base_starts = [
        best_first,
        threshold_h(best_first, best_first_n),
        make_random_h(rng, best_first_n),
        make_tm_h(best_first_n),
        make_golden_h(rng, best_first_n),
        make_sinusoidal_h(rng, best_first_n),
    ]

    if remaining_time() > 70.0:
        bl = threshold_h(best_first, best_first_n)
        h_bin0, c_bin0 = binary_search(bl, best_first_n,
                                       min(35.0, max(15.0, remaining_time() * 0.08)), rng)
        if math.isfinite(c_bin0) and c_bin0 < best_c5:
            best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h_bin0, len(h_bin0), c_bin0
            print("binary base improved: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))
        base_starts.append(h_bin0)

    base_tasks = [(best_first_n, s) for s in base_starts]
    for (nn, start) in base_tasks:
        if remaining_time() < 90.0:
            break
        h_norm, cn = precondition_candidate(start if len(start) == nn else interpolate_h(start, nn))
        default_tl = 60.0 if cn <= 320 else 110.0
        tl = min(default_tl, max(15.0, remaining_time() * 0.20))
        tl = min(tl, max(10.0, remaining_time() - 90.0))
        if tl < 10.0:
            continue
        h2, c2 = optimize_from_start(h_norm, cn, tl, rng)
        if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h2, len(h2), c2
            print("base improve: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

    n2 = 512
    if remaining_time() > 120.0:
        if best_n == n2:
            h20 = project_h(best_h, 0.5 * float(n2), 80)
        else:
            h20, c20 = precondition_candidate(interpolate_h(best_h, n2))
        if math.isfinite(c20) and c20 < best_c5:
            best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h20, len(h20), c20

        starts2 = [
            h20,
            threshold_h(h20, n2),
            make_tm_h(n2),
            make_random_h(rng, n2),
            make_golden_h(rng, n2),
        ]

        if remaining_time() > 90.0:
            h_bin2, c_bin2 = binary_search(threshold_h(h20, n2), n2,
                                           min(30.0, max(12.0, remaining_time() * 0.07)), rng)
            if math.isfinite(c_bin2) and c_bin2 < best_c5:
                best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h_bin2, len(h_bin2), c_bin2
                print("binary 512 improved: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))
            starts2.append(h_bin2)

        for (nn, start) in starts2:
            if remaining_time() < 100.0:
                break
            hl, cl = precondition_candidate(start if len(start) == nn else interpolate_h(start, nn))
            default_tl = 100.0 if cl <= 320 else 130.0
            tl = min(default_tl, max(20.0, remaining_time() * 0.22))
            tl = min(tl, max(12.0, remaining_time() - 100.0))
            if tl < 12.0:
                continue
            h2, c2 = optimize_from_start(hl, cl, tl, rng)
            if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h2, len(h2), c2
                print("512 improve: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

    if remaining_time() > 320.0 and best_n < 768:
        n3 = 768
        h3, c3 = precondition_candidate(interpolate_h(best_h, n3))
        if math.isfinite(c3) and c3 < best_c5:
            best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h3, len(h3), c3
            print("768 interp improved: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

        starts3 = [h3, threshold_h(h3, n3)]
        for (nn, start) in starts3:
            if remaining_time() < 140.0:
                break
            default_tl = 140.0
            tl = min(default_tl, max(20.0, remaining_time() * 0.25))
            tl = min(tl, max(15.0, remaining_time() - 130.0))
            if tl < 15.0:
                continue
            h2, c2 = optimize_from_start(start, nn, tl, rng)
            if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h2, len(h2), c2
                print("768 improve: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

    if remaining_time() > 70.0 and best_n <= 384:
        h_p, c_p = polish_slsqp(best_h, best_n,
                                min(55.0, max(20.0, remaining_time() * 0.45)),
                                (500.0, 2000.0))
        if math.isfinite(c_p) and c_p < best_c5:
            best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h_p, len(h_p), c_p
            print("slsqp polish: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

    if remaining_time() > 60.0 and best_n <= 512:
        h_p, c_p = polish_projected_gradient(best_h, best_n,
                                             min(45.0, max(18.0, remaining_time() * 0.45)),
                                             beta=900.0)
        if math.isfinite(c_p) and c_p < best_c5:
            best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h_p, len(h_p), c_p
            print("projected-gradient polish: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

    if remaining_time() > 45.0:
        h_p, c_p = optimize_from_start(best_h, best_n,
                                       min(45.0, max(15.0, remaining_time() * 0.55)),
                                       rng,
                                       betas=(500.0, 1500.0))
        if math.isfinite(c_p) and c_p < best_c5:
            best_h, best_n, best_c5 = h_p, len(h_p), c_p
            print("final L-BFGS polish: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, best_c5))

    best_h = force_exact_sum(best_h)
    final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        eval_c5 = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if math.isfinite(eval_c5) and eval_c5 < final_c5:
            final_c5 = float(eval_c5)
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_n = len(best_h)
    print("FINAL: n={}, C5={:.8f}".format(best_n, final_c5))
    return (best_h, final_c5, best_n)
```
